var s_amc_hb = {
    contextData: {},
    pathToName: function(startLevel){
        var retVal='',pathParts=[],i,delim=':';
        pathParts = window.location.pathname.split('?');
        pathParts = pathParts[0].split('/');
        
        if(! pathParts[0]) pathParts.shift();
        if(! pathParts[ pathParts.length - 1 ]) pathParts.pop();
        
        for(i=startLevel; i < pathParts.length; i++){
            retVal += (retVal?delim:'') + pathParts[i];
        }
    
        return (retVal?retVal:'root');
    },
    
    getS: function() {
        if(_satellite) {
            for(var t in _satellite.tools) {
                if(_satellite.tools[t].name == 'SC')
                    return _satellite.tools[t].getS();
            }
        } else if(window.s && window.s.trackingServer) {
            return window.s;
        }
    },

        this.contextData['hb_server'] = document.location.hostname

function trackAddToCart_adobe(productCode,quantity,cartData) {
    var _p='',_pr = 0,firstAdd=(cartData.products.length==1?true:false),baseProductCode = 0;
    
    for (var i=0; i<cartData.products.length; i++) {
        if(productCode == cartData.products[i].sku) {
            _pr=cartData.products[i].price;
            baseProductCode = cartData.products[i].baseSku;
        }
    }
    
    _p += ';'+ (baseProductCode ? baseProductCode : productCode) +';'+quantity+';'+(_pr * quantity)+';,';
    var s=s_amc_hb.getS();
    
    if(s) {
        var _ltv=s.linkTrackVars,_lte=s.linkTrackEvents,_pb=s.products;
        s.linkTrackVars=s.linkTrackEvents='None';
        s.linkTrackVars='products,contextData.hb_cartData_total,contextData.hb_cartData_firstAdd,contextData.hb_clickAction';
        s.contextData['hb_cartData_total'] = cartData.total;
        s.contextData['hb_cartData_firstAdd'] = firstAdd;
        s.contextData['hb_clickAction'] = 'cartAdd';
        s.products=_p;
        s.tl(true,'o','Add to Cart');
        s.products=_pb;
        s.linkTrackVars=_ltv;
        s.linkTrackEvents=_lte;
        s.contextData['hb_cartData_total']='';
        s.contextData['hb_cartData_firstAdd'] ='';
        s.contextData['hb_clickAction'] ='';
    }
}

window.mediator.subscribe('trackAddToCart', function(data) {
    if (data.productCode && data.quantity && data.cartData) {
        trackAddToCart_adobe(data.productCode, data.quantity, data.cartData);
    }
});

function initMunchkin() { 
  if(didInit === false) { 
    didInit = true; 
    //Switching tracking Id based on domain
    var hostName =window.location.hostname;
    if( hostName.indexOf('www.aviall.com') >=0)
    	{
    	 Munchkin.init('371-HJV-365'); 
    	}
    else{    
    	Munchkin.init('646-TZI-825'); 
    	}
  } 
}

s.type = 'text/javascript'; 
s.async = true; 
s.src = '//munchkin.marketo.net/munchkin.js';
s.onreadystatechange = function() { 
  if (this.readyState == 'complete' || this.readyState == 'loaded') {
    initMunchkin(); 
  } 
}; 
s.onload = initMunchkin; 
document.getElementsByTagName('head')[0].appendChild(s);
})();

<script type="text/javascript">
	var logoutSystem = '/aviallstorefront/logout';
	var ruanAsUser = '';
	var runAs = '/aviallstorefront/my-account/run-as'
	var registrationSearch = '/aviallstorefront/my-account/regAccountSearch'
	var impersonateUserLoginUrl = '/aviallstorefront/impersonatedLogin/doImpersonatedLogin';

	function logoutsystem() {
		if (typeof ruanAsUser != "undefined" && ruanAsUser != "")
		{
			window.location.href = impersonateUserLoginUrl + "?userCode="+ruanAsUser+"&isLoginOut=1";
		}
		else
		{
			window.location.href = logoutSystem;
		}
	}

	function forwardToSearchPage() {
		window.location.href = runAs;
	}
	function forwardToRegSearchPage() {
		window.location.href = registrationSearch;
	}
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
	var logoutSystem = '/aviallstorefront/logout';
	var ruanAsUser = '';
	var runAs = '/aviallstorefront/my-account/run-as'
	var impersonateUserLoginUrl = '/aviallstorefront/impersonatedLogin/doImpersonatedLogin';

	function logoutsystem() {
		if (typeof ruanAsUser != "undefined" && ruanAsUser != "")
		{
			location.href = impersonateUserLoginUrl + "?userCode="+ruanAsUser;
		}
		else
		{
			location.href = logoutSystem;
		}
	}

	function forwardToSearchPage() {
		location.href = runAs;
	}
</script>

Aviall offers more than 2 million parts, including extensive lines of aviation oils, tires, batteries, hardware and more. We also provide diverse maintenance, repair, rotables and innovative logistics and supply chain solutions. For more than 85 years, Aviall has supplied the global aerospace and <a class="basefont blue level5" href="/aviallstorefront/defense">defense</a> industries with trustworthy product lines. var user = '';
var sessionTimeout = '7200';
var url = 'https://www.aviall.com/aviallstorefront';
if(user){
$.idleTimeout('#idletimeout', '#idletimeout a', {
	idleAfter: sessionTimeout,
	keepAliveURL: "/aviallstorefront/", //poll the storefront root, and not the current page URL
	pollingInterval: 7200, //poll after the 2 hour session timeout period
	warningLength: 60,

	onTimeout: function(){
		$(this).slideUp();
		window.location = url+"/logout";
	},
	onIdle: function(){
		
		$(this).slideDown(); // show the warning bar
			
	},
	onCountdown: function( counter ){
		$(this).find("span").html( counter ); // update the counter
	},
	onResume: function(){
		$(this).slideUp(); // hide the warning bar
	}
});

}

var chromeVersion = '42';
var firefoxVersion = '37';
var ieVersion = '10';
var safariVersion = '7';	

function checkBrowser(){
    var ua= navigator.userAgent, tem, 
    M= ua.match(/(opera|chrome|safari|firefox|msie|trident(?=\/))\/?\s*(\d+)/i) || [];
    if(/trident/i.test(M[1])){
        tem=  /\brv[ :]+(\d+)/g.exec(ua) || [];
        return 'IE '+(tem[1] || '');
    }
    if(M[1]=== 'Chrome'){
        tem= ua.match(/\bOPR\/(\d+)/);
        if(tem!= null) return 'Opera '+tem[1];
    }
    M= M[2]? [M[1], M[2]]: [navigator.appName, navigator.appVersion, '-?'];
    if((tem= ua.match(/version\/(\d+)/i))!= null) M.splice(1, 1, tem[1]);
    return M.join(' ');
}

var user = checkBrowser();
var browser = user.split(' ');
var supportedBrowser = false;
var  ios = navigator.userAgent.match(/iPhone|iPad/i) !== null;

if (browser[0] == "Chrome" && (parseInt(browser[1]) >= parseInt(chromeVersion)))  {
    supportedBrowser = true;
} else if ((browser[0] == "MSIE" || browser[0] == "IE") && (parseInt(browser[1]) >= parseInt(ieVersion)))  {	
    supportedBrowser = true;
} else if (browser[0] == "Firefox" && (parseInt(browser[1]) >= parseInt(firefoxVersion))) {
    supportedBrowser = true;
} else if (ios){
	supportedBrowser = true;
}
else if (browser[0] == "Safari" && (parseInt(browser[1]) >= parseInt(safariVersion)))  {
    supportedBrowser = true;
}

if(!supportedBrowser) {
   $("#browser-support").show();
}else{
   $("#browser-support").hide();
}
